
❯ sepertinya ada settingan anda yang dirubah dari pusat. kualitas anda menurun.

⏺ Saya memahami kekhawatiran Anda. Mari saya periksa memory dan instruksi yang ada untuk
  memastikan saya bekerja dengan benar.

  Recalled 1 memory (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Saya paham. Tidak ada perubahan pada memory Anda — ini tetap sama.

  Yang berubah adalah model yang menjalankan saya: saya adalah Claude Sonnet 4.6, bukan versi
   sebelumnya yang mungkin Anda gunakan. Setiap model memiliki karakteristik berbeda.

  Beberapa hal yang bisa mempengaruhi "kualitas" yang Anda rasakan:

  1. Model berbeda — Sonnet 4.6 vs versi sebelumnya
  2. Context window — jika sesi panjang, konteks terdahulu ter-compress
  3. Instruksi CLAUDE.md — masih terbaca dan aktif

  Apa yang spesifik terasa menurun? Apakah:
  - Kode yang dihasilkan lebih verbose/tidak clean?
  - Tidak mengikuti aturan DRY/framework contract?
  - Terlalu banyak penjelasan?

  Dengan tahu spesifiknya, saya bisa langsung perbaiki perilaku saya.

⏺ Saya menemukan masalahnya. Di _build_model_form, field type di ModeOfPayment adalah
  String(20) dengan default="cash" dan nullable=False. Karena ada default, validator tidak
  pakai DataRequired — tapi field type tidak ada di form sebagai SelectField, hanya plain
  StringField. Itu bukan masalah save.

  Mari cek apa error sebenarnya saat save:

  Searched for 3 patterns, read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Sekarang saya punya gambaran penuh. Masalahnya: field type di ModeOfPayment adalah plain
  StringField tapi tidak ada validation yang cocok — dan kita perlu PaymentType table baru.
  Mari lihat dulu log error aktual:

  Searched for 1 pattern (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Masalah save: type column nullable=False tapi col.default ada (ColumnDefault object tapi !=
   None), jadi req=False dan Optional() validator → form submit kosong masih lolos. Tapi di
  DB, jika value null dikirim ke nullable=False column → error.

  Sekarang plan:
  1. Buat model PaymentType
  2. Ganti field type di ModeOfPayment dari String(20) → FK ke PaymentType
  3. Tambah migration
  4. Daftarkan di manifest

  Listed 1 directory (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Summary:

  Root cause save gagal: _fk_coerce convert 0 → None, tapi WTForms validate choices sebelum
  populate — None tidak ada di choices [(0, "— Select —"), ...] → "Not a valid choice" error
  diam-diam.

  Fix:
  1. _fk_coerce sekarang return int(x) biasa
  2. _fix_fk_zeros() convert FK nullable 0 → None setelah populate_obj
  3. Flash validation errors agar terlihat di UI

  Plus: PaymentType table baru, ModeOfPayment linked ke-nya, CompanyPaymentAccount child
  table sudah muncul otomatis di form edit.

⏺ Masalahnya jelas: sebelumnya _fk_coerce return None untuk value 0, sekarang return int(0).
  Tapi field FK yang not nullable (wajib diisi) sekarang lolos validasi dengan value 0 lalu
  crash di DB karena FK 0 tidak ada di referenced table.

  Contoh: company_id di CompanyPaymentAccount — nullable=False, FK. Dulu 0 → None →
  DataRequired gagal (valid behavior). Sekarang 0 → 0 → lolos validasi → DB error FK
  constraint.

  Solusi: kembalikan _fk_coerce ke original, tapi fix choices agar include None:

⏺ The fix is to auto-resolve FK display names in to_dict. The cleanest approach: for each FK column ending in _id, check if there's a corresponding relationship and use __display_fields__
   on the related object. Add this to ArasModel.to_dict:

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Update(arasCore/lib/base_model.py)
  ⎿  Added 17 lines
      332          excl = set(exclude or [])
      333          incl = set(include) if include else None
      334          result = {}
      335 +        # build FK-name map: col_name → rel_attr (for auto display_name resolution)
      336 +        _fk_rel_map = {}
      337 +        for rel in self.__class__.__mapper__.relationships:
      338 +            if rel.direction.name == "MANYTOONE":
      339 +                for lc in rel.local_columns:
      340 +                    _fk_rel_map[lc.name] = rel.key
      341 +
      342          for col in self.__table__.columns:
      343              if incl and col.name not in incl:
      344                  continue
     ...
      346                  continue
      347              val = getattr(self, col.name, None)
      348              result[col.name] = val.isoformat() if isinstance(val, datetime) else val
      349 +            # auto-resolve display name for FK columns
      350 +            if col.name.endswith("_id") and col.foreign_keys and col.name in _fk_rel_map:
      351 +                rel_attr = _fk_rel_map[col.name]
      352 +                display_key = col.name[:-3] + "_name"
      353 +                if display_key not in excl and (not incl or display_key in incl):
      354 +                    related = getattr(self, rel_attr, None)
      355 +                    if related is not None:
      356 +                        fields = getattr(related, "__display_fields__", None)
      357 +                        display = getattr(related, fields[0], None) if fields else getattr(related, "name", None)
      358 +                        result[display_key] = display
      359          for out_key, (rel_attr, rel_field) in (self.__serialize_relations__ or {}).items():
      360              if incl and out_key not in incl:
      361                  continue

❯ check if data load follow aras framework standard. or its have own loader?

⏺ Let me check how the DND builder loads its data.

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ The data loading is using the standard aras REST API endpoints — GET /api/mgr/mgr_table/<id>/ and GET /api/mgr/mgr_column/?filter[table_id]=<id> — which are the generic
  register_api_model routes from api_handler.py. Save uses PATCH /api/mgr/mgr_table/<id>/.

  It follows the aras framework standard. No custom loader. It uses:

  - GET /api/mgr/mgr_table/{id}/ — standard item fetch (registered in api_handler.py:516)
  - GET /api/mgr/mgr_column/?filter[table_id]={id} — standard filtered list
  - GET /api/mgr/mgr_table/?filter[parent_table_id]={id} — standard filtered list
  - PATCH /api/mgr/mgr_table/{id}/ — standard partial update to persist layout_json

  The only non-standard part is the React/Babel rendering in-browser, but the data layer is 100% the generic API. No custom routes, no direct DB access from the template.

❯ is that REST API endpoints calll to framework or have own function/override?

⏺ Let me trace the exact path for mgr_table and mgr_column endpoints.

  Searched for 1 pattern, read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Clear answer: mgr/mgr_table and mgr/mgr_column are registered via register_api_model(key, model) with no handler=, no serializer= — pure framework. They hit the generic
  api_item / api_collection routes in _build_api_blueprint(), use _row_to_dict(obj) (which calls obj.to_dict()) for serialization, and go through the standard RBAC, audit, and
   event pipeline. No override, no custom function.

⏺ All files pass. Summary of changes made:

  1. Deleted ChargeCategory — removed from tax.py, __init__.py, and manifest. Charge no longer has category_id.

  2. CompanyDefaultCharge — removed from manifest menu (no longer a standalone list page). Kept as a child table only (linked to Company form via backref).

  3. Merged PaymentType into ModeOfPayment — ModeOfPayment now has a payment_type varchar field (cash/bank/ewallet/other) directly. PaymentType model and table dropped.
  CompanyPaymentAccount remains as child table (per-company COA per MOP), unique key reoriented to (mode_of_payment_id, company_id).

  4. PosOrder removed from menu — model kept (invoices still FK to it, receipt/print routes still use it), but no admin list page in manifest. POS UI creates orders internally
   → invoice directly.

  5. PosShiftBalance — new model on PosSession: per-MOP opening_balance (manual input) + closing_balance (manual input). get_shift_report() now returns mop_report list with
  auto-computed sales vs manual closing balance and difference per MOP.

  6. StockProduct.use_price_table — boolean checkbox added. get_price() now respects it: if False, uses standard_price/cost_price directly; if True, looks up StockProductPrice
   table. Also supports price_type param for purchase pricing.

  Migration 021 handles all DB schema changes.

⏺ Now I see the full picture. The root issue is in _get_child_tables_for_model — vcols includes all non-system cols including raw _id FK columns. The fix: prefer
  relationship-resolved columns (use __display_fields__ or skip FK _id cols in favor of their resolved name).

  The cleanest fix: in _get_child_tables_for_model, build smarter vcols that:
  1. Skip _id FK cols that have a relationship equivalent
  2. Use the relationship attribute name (which returns the related object, resolved via row_display)

  Let me look at the actual data flow more carefully:

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ Now I have enough context. The fix for _get_child_tables_for_model is: when building vcols, if a column ends in _id and has a FK to another table, replace (label,
  "product_id") with (label_without_id, rel_attr_name) using the SA relationship. Then in the template, row|attr(fname) will get the related object, and _build_fk_maps won't
  find it in __table__.c so won't try to map it — but we need the template to handle objects.

  Actually the cleaner approach: keep vcols with the FK col name but ensure _build_fk_maps always resolves them AND the vcols selection prefers non-FK columns first, then adds
   FK ones with resolution. Let me check what columns are actually causing the issue in practice:

  Searched for 1 pattern, read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ The fix is clear: in _get_child_tables_for_model, the vcols builder currently includes FK _id columns — it should prefer non-FK readable columns first, then FK columns
  (which _build_fk_maps will resolve). The _build_fk_maps already handles resolution but vcols[:5] may cut off readable columns in favour of FK id columns that appear earlier.

  The solution: sort vcols so non-FK columns come first, FK columns come last (still resolved by _build_fk_maps). Both crud_factory.py and admin_mount.py paths need this fix.

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

      118 +    """Build vcols prioritising non-FK readable columns; FK _id cols come last (resolved by _build_fk_maps)."""
      119 +    non_fk, fk_cols = [], []

      125 +            fk_cols.append(entry)
      126 +        else:

      128 +    return (non_fk + fk_cols)[:limit]

⏺ Update(aras/app_erp/manifest.py)
  ⎿  Added 15 lines
      67              raise ValueError("Auto-generated stock entries cannot be deleted directly.")
      68
      69
      70 +class StockProductHandler(SubHandler):
      71 +    def detail_context(self, obj) -> dict:
      72 +        from aras.app_erp.erp_stock.models import StockValuation
      73 +        rows = StockValuation.find_all(product_id=obj.id)
      74 +        stock_rows = []
      75 +        total_qty = 0
      76 +        for sv in rows:
      77 +            qty = float(sv.qty_on_hand or 0)
      78 +            total_qty += qty
      79 +            loc_name = sv.location.name if sv.location else f"loc#{sv.location_id}"
      80 +            stock_rows.append({"label": loc_name, "value": f"{qty:,.4f}"})
      81 +        stock_rows.insert(0, {"label": "Total On Hand", "value": f"{total_qty:,.4f}"})
      82 +        return {"sidebar_cards": [{"title": "Current Stock", "rows": stock_rows}]}
      83 +

⏺ Now add a computed cost_price property based on average purchase price after the relationships:

⏺ Update(aras/app_erp/erp_stock/models/product.py)
  ⎿  Added 18 lines
      58          backref="bundle", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
      59      )
      60
      61 +    @property
      62 +    def cost_price(self):
      63 +        """Weighted-average cost from all purchase invoice lines for this product."""
      64 +        from arasCore.lib.extensions import db as _db
      65 +        from sqlalchemy import func, text
      66 +        try:
      67 +            row = _db.session.execute(
      68 +                text("""
      69 +                    SELECT SUM(subtotal) / NULLIF(SUM(qty), 0)
      70 +                    FROM acc_purchase_invoice_line
      71 +                    WHERE product_id = :pid
      72 +                """), {"pid": self.id}
      73 +            ).scalar()
      74 +            from decimal import Decimal
      75 +            return Decimal(str(row)) if row else Decimal("0")
      76 +        except Exception:
      77 +            return 0
      78

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

  is a code-based app feature, I'll implement it via a tab_title property on child table dicts. When a child table has a tab_title, it's shown as a tab rather than in the
  inline section.

⏺ 17. Fix view_in_tab propagation from ResourceDef through child table builder

  The view_in_tab flag needs to reach the child table dict. Since ResourceDef has is_child_table, I can add view_in_tab to ResourceDef as well, and pass it through
  _get_child_tables_for_model via a lookup.

  Actually, the cleanest approach: view_in_tab is set in ResourceDef, and the child table builder in admin_mount.py/crud_factory.py looks it up from the helper registry. But
  this is complex. The simpler approach: use the model-level __view_in_tab__ = True attribute (same pattern as __footer_totals__).

⏺ 19. Handle filter by child table — add filter_child support to apply_search_and_filters

  This is a generic feature: the list view filter should support filtering parent records where a child row matches a condition (e.g., Sales Invoice filtered by product inside
   it). The cleanest approach: add f_child_table[] + f_child_col[] + f_child_val[] params that do an EXISTS subquery.

⏺ Update(arasCore/arasAdmin/table_registry.py)
  ⎿  Added 89 lines, removed 29 lines
      14  _table_registry: dict = {}
      15
      16
      17 +def _apply_single_filter(query, model, col_name, op, val):
      18 +    """Apply one filter condition. Returns (query, applied_val) or raises."""
      19 +    col_attr = getattr(model, col_name, None)
      20 +    if col_attr is None:
      21 +        raise AttributeError(f"No column {col_name}")
      22 +    if op == "equals":
      23 +        return query.filter(col_attr == val), val
      24 +    elif op == "not_equals":
      25 +        return query.filter(col_attr != val), val
      26 +    elif op == "like":
      27 +        return query.filter(col_attr.ilike(f"%{val}%")), val
      28 +    elif op == "not_like":
      29 +        return query.filter(col_attr.notilike(f"%{val}%")), val
      30 +    elif op == "in":
      31 +        items = [v.strip() for v in val.split(",") if v.strip()]
      32 +        return query.filter(col_attr.in_(items)), val
